## Mandate

This lane delivers, in `apps/web` ONLY, the whole client side of three v4
commits at `236c04e35`: (1) the punctuation-lint ENGINE + prose-mode
predicate as a TS port that imports nothing, byte-identical in behaviour to
v4's `lib/punctuation-lint/{engine,prose-mode}.ts` over the two byte-copied
vector corpora AND a recorded corpus executed out of v4's real engine at the
pin (ruling R-I); (2) the composer adapter as a ProseMirror
`Decoration.inline` plugin (a RECORDED adapter divergence — v4 paints with
the CSS Custom Highlight API; the four `::highlight(name)` rules become
`.name` class rules carrying the SAME tokens), its `punctuationLint` input
on `qt-rich-editor`, the `proseMode` / `markdownAsterisks` inputs on
`qt-chat-composer`, the toolbar's Bold withhold, the settings card's fourth
toggle reading `punctuationWarnings ?? true` (C7), and the
`SmartTypographySettings` service's third signal; (3) the Novel template's
SPA half — the template editor whole (the third narration radio, the
`Unmarked Text` section, the shared `AddOnsEditor`, the two `wrap`
checkboxes, the `Plain` style option, the form helpers' `null` narration /
`unmarkedStyle` / `markdownAsterisks` / `matchCurlyQuotes` / `closeOptional`
round-trips, the widened contract types in your `core-contract.ts` fence)
AND the Novel renderer in `chat/render/*` (the five-scope wire narrowing —
FIXING a v5-only defect — the nested tokenizer with `children`, the
`unmarked` class on direct block text through an open-tag stack, the
`strip` / `literal` pre-parse, `stripUnmarkedSpans` under a line rule, the
`mentionsGlyph` exemption), proven by RE-CAPTURING `markdown-fixtures.json`
from the pin with the Novel rows of survey §5.3. It must NOT touch: any
crate (`crates/**`, `harness/**` beyond ONE new recorder file under
`harness/oracle/cases/`); `help/`; `chat/message-content.ts` /
`chat/message-row.ts` / `chat/streaming-message.ts` (P4.D353 — it
post-passes the HTML your `renderMarkdownToHtml` returns, so that function's
signature and the `render-cache.ts` key stay as they are);
`screens/salon/salon-conversation.ts` + its spec (P4.D344 — HANDOFF H-2);
any EXISTING line of `_variables.css` / `_chat.css` (P4.D357 — fenced
appends only); `editor/markdown-field.ts` (NOT claimed — P4.D358's);
`apps/web/package.json`'s dependency lists and `package-lock.json` beyond
the version bump (P4.D353 adds the diagram deps); `_index.css` (P4.D344 /
P4.D346's `CssIndexImports`); `chat-settings.types.ts`'s `AUTOMATION_
OPTIONS` region (P4.D346). `qt-roleplay-plain` gets NO CSS rule anywhere
(ruling R-6). Every string the user sees is v4's byte (quoted below or
pointed at by `path:line` at the pin); nothing is paraphrased.

## Survey-verified starting points (2026-10-29, at `236c04e35`; v5 `main` `f6cdceedd`)

Everything below was MEASURED (the survey's §2.2 + this order's own reads),
not inferred. Re-measure a line only if `main` moved under you (the
orders' commit may differ from `f6cdceedd` by the planner's own docs
commits — source lines should be unmoved; verify the first three anchors
you touch and record any drift).

  `stray-closing-quote`, `floating-quote`, `glued-quote`, `backwards-curly-

  {block, start, end, kind}`; `:94-107` `PUNCTUATION_RULE_MESSAGES` — the
  nine hover sentences, byte-exact, e.g. `'nested-double-quote': 'A double
  quote inside dialogue that is already open. Quoting someone inside
  speech? Use single quotes.'` … `'no-dialogue': 'No dialogue yet — not a
  single opening quote in the whole draft. Meant to say this aloud? Put the
  speech in double quotes.'`; `:396` `classifyProse(blocks)`; `:413`
  `lintPunctuation(blocks, options)`). Internals (`:109-120` the quote

  blank lines, a `null` block is a barrier; `:171` `quoteShape`; `:199`
  `lintQuotes` → `{issues, unclosedOpener, opensWithQuote, dialogue}`; `:255`
  `caretAfter`; `:259` `lintAsterisks`; `:306` `lintOoc`; `:331` `oocSpans`;
  `:359` `classifyParagraph`). **Port by COPY, then convert the file to the

  {narrationDelimiters?: unknown; delimiters?: readonly {kind?: string;

- **`chat/render/roleplay-rendering.ts`** (352 lines): `RenderingPattern.

  non-`'line'` scope to `'inline'`** — its own doc comment (`:45-50`) says
  "this only moves v4's own coercion one step earlier", which was true at
  `322a5488e` and is FALSE at the pin: v4's `compileRenderingPatterns`
  (`$PIN/lib/chat/roleplay-rendering.ts:110-118`) now FILTERS OUT `literal`
  / `strip` and maps `unmarked` to its own scope. Through today's v5
  narrowing the Novel template's `[\s\S]` unmarked pattern becomes an
  INLINE rule claiming every character, and `\*{2,}` / `\*` become inline
  rules too (survey §0 item 2). `Segment :103-106` (no `children`);
  `CompiledRule :109-114` (`scope: 'inline' | 'line'`); `compileRendering
  Patterns :120-127`; `tokenizeInline :133-171` (flat); `segmentsToHtml
  :262-268` (flat); `MARKDOWN_CHARS :271`; `hasRpBodyGroup :273`;
  `escapeRpBodyInterior :277-296`; `escapeMarkdownInBrackets :299-352` (no
  `strip` / `literal`; the early return lacks the two new length checks).
- **`chat/render/markdown-renderer.ts`** (351 lines; v5 has NO
  `markdown-postprocess.ts` — the Ownership row names one; the post-pass
  functions live HERE and stay here, ruling R-9): `applyRoleplayPatterns
  :90-131` — the tag split, `inCodeBlock`, AND the v5-only `katexDepth`
  guard (`:93-115`; v4's server post-pass at the pin has it too —

  thread the `openTags` stack beside it without disturbing it: v4 calls
  `trackOpenTags(openTags, lowerPart)` AFTER the katex / code bookkeeping,

  `applyWrapBlockClasses :204-230`; `renderMarkdownToHtml :297-351` — the

  applyWrapBlockClasses → applyRoleplayPatterns → applyLineScopedClasses →

  service.ts` and record the line). `chat/render/render-cache.ts:57-77` —
  the memo key (`content + patterns ref + detection ref + blobMountPointId

  Novel behaviour rides the patterns reference already in it).
- **`chat/render/typography.ts`** (159 lines): `STRAIGHT_QUOTES :74`,

  `if (pattern.pattern.includes(quote)) return true;` with NO
  `mentionsGlyph` exemption, so Novel's dialogue pattern (`["“](?<rpBody>
  [^"”]+)(?:["”]|$)` — it has an `rpBody` group AND contains `"`) makes
  `shouldCurlQuotes` answer `false` for every Novel chat with
  `displayQuotes` ON. v4 at the pin (`$PIN/lib/markdown/typography.ts:
  89-96`): `mentionsGlyph(source, glyph) = source.includes(glyph) ||
  source.toLowerCase().includes('\\u' + glyph.charCodeAt(0).toString(16).

  `CURLY_FORMS[quote].every((c) => mentionsGlyph(pattern.pattern, c))`.

- **The v4 hunks to port (pin lines):** `roleplay-rendering.ts:68-74`
  (`Segment.children`), `:85-93` (`CompiledRule.scope` three-valued),

  `:102-105` (`UNMARKED_BLOCK_TAGS = {p, li, blockquote, h1..h6}`,

  a closing tag truncates the stack at the LAST same-name entry; a void or

- `styles/qt-components/_chat.css:1042-1044` — the `/** Lexical rich text
  node styles */` banner; your four CLASS rules go immediately BEFORE it, in

  ```
  /* ==== P4.D352: punctuation-lint decorations (v4 _chat.css:1047-1075 ::highlight() rules as class rules) ==== */
  .qt-composer-narration { color: var(--qt-composer-narration-fg); background-color: var(--qt-composer-narration-bg); }
  .qt-composer-ooc { color: var(--qt-composer-ooc-fg); background-color: var(--qt-composer-ooc-bg); }
  .qt-punctuation-no-dialogue { background-color: var(--qt-punctuation-no-dialogue-bg); text-decoration: underline dotted var(--qt-punctuation-no-dialogue-underline); text-decoration-thickness: 2px; }
  .qt-punctuation-warning { background-color: var(--qt-punctuation-warning-bg); text-decoration: underline wavy var(--qt-punctuation-warning-underline); text-decoration-thickness: 2px; }
  ```

## Tiered deliverables

Numbering is the commit order that keeps every intermediate tree green
(`npm run lint && npm run build && npx vitest run` after each). Each item
names its red-first measurement (§R.7 — "a test that passes before the fix
is a test of nothing"); record every count in the lane record.

3. **The ProseMirror adapter (the RECORDED adapter divergence — ruling
   R-2).** NEW `apps/web/src/app/editor/punctuation-lint-plugin.ts`
   exporting `punctuationLintPlugin(inputs: () => PunctuationLintInputs |
   null): Plugin` and `interface PunctuationLintInputs { enabled: boolean;
   proseMode: boolean }`, plus the four class-name constants with v4's
   bytes (`PUNCTUATION_HIGHLIGHT_NAME = 'qt-punctuation-warning'`,
   `NO_DIALOGUE_HIGHLIGHT_NAME = 'qt-punctuation-no-dialogue'`,
   `NARRATION_HIGHLIGHT_NAME = 'qt-composer-narration'`,
   `OOC_HIGHLIGHT_NAME = 'qt-composer-ooc'`) and the priority table
   (3 / 2 / 1 / 0 — now the ORDER decorations are added and the CSS cascade
   order, S.7). Shape — "every decision lives in the engine; this file only
   turns the editor into the engine's input and the engine's offsets back
   into ranges" (v4's header, kept):
   - **`collectBlocks(doc)`** — the twin of `$collectBlocks` over the
     ProseMirror doc: walk `doc.descendants`; every TEXTBLOCK node
     (`paragraph`, `heading`, the paragraph inside a `list_item` — list
     items are not textblocks in this schema) is one block `{text, start:
     pos + 1}`; a `code_block` is `{text: null}`; inside a textblock, a text
     node under the `code` mark contributes `'x'.repeat(n)`, a `hard_break`
     contributes `'\n'`, any other inline LEAF (image, a mention atom)
     contributes `'￼'` — each ONE character per ONE position, so block
     offset ↔ doc position is `start + offset` with no segment table (the
     Lexical adapter needed one because its keys are not positions; record
     this simplification); an EMPTY textblock still yields `''` ("it is
     where the caret may be").
   - **`caretFor(state, blocks)`** — a collapsed `TextSelection` only (else
     `null`); find the block whose `[start, start + text.length]` contains
     `$from.pos`; `offset = pos - start`.
   - **`paint(state, inputs)`** — v4's `paint` gate verbatim: `if (!enabled
     && !proseMode) return {explained: [], decorations: DecorationSet.
     empty}`; when `enabled`, each `lintPunctuation(texts, {caret,
     proseMode})` issue → `Decoration.inline(start + s, start + e, {class:
     name, title: PUNCTUATION_RULE_MESSAGES[issue.rule]})` under
     `qt-punctuation-no-dialogue` when `rule === 'no-dialogue'`, else
     `qt-punctuation-warning` (an issue in a `null` block never arrives —
     the engine skips them; an issue whose range falls outside the block is
     dropped, v4's `rangeFor` null arm); when `proseMode`, each
     `classifyProse(texts)` span → `Decoration.inline(…, {class: kind ===
     'ooc' ? OOC : NARRATION})` with NO title. Build the `DecorationSet`
     with decorations added in ASCENDING priority (narration, ooc,
     no-dialogue, warning) so a same-range pair stacks the warning innermost
     — MEASURE in Chromium how ProseMirror nests two inline decorations over
     one range (record the DOM; the CSS cascade in S.7 covers the merged-
     attributes case either way).
   - **The plugin:** `new Plugin({ key: punctuationLintKey, state: { init:
     (_, state) => paint(state, inputs()), apply: (tr, prev, _old, state) =>
     tr.docChanged || tr.selectionSet || tr.getMeta(punctuationLintKey) ===
     'refresh' ? paint(state, inputs()) : prev }, props: { decorations:
     (state) => key.getState(state).decorations } })`. The host
     (`rich-editor.ts`) dispatches `tr.setMeta(punctuationLintKey,
     'refresh')` from an `effect` that reads the `punctuationLint` input
     (the `smartTypography` input is read live in a closure; this one must
     REPAINT when the toggle flips with no keystroke — v4's effect re-runs
     on `[editor, enabled, proseMode]`). The three console lines, bytes
     kept: `console.debug('[punctuation-lint] mounted', { warnings:
     enabled, proseMode })` on install and on every input change (v4's
     effect re-mount); `` console.debug(`[punctuation-lint] ${count}
     warning(s)`) `` ONLY when the number of EXPLAINED issues changes
     ("never per keystroke"); the `CSS Custom Highlight API unavailable;
     warnings disabled` line is NOT ported (the adapter needs no API —
     recorded, Tier 3 item 2). Hover: the per-decoration `title` attribute
     IS the explanation (v4 sets the root's `title` from
     `caretPositionFromPoint` because a `Highlight` has no element; a
     decoration span does — recorded, Tier 3 item 3); specific-before-
     general hover order is moot per element and is recorded with it.
   - **`editor/rich-editor.ts`:** a new input beside `smartTypography`
     (`:96-102`): `readonly punctuationLint = input<PunctuationLintInputs |
     null>(null);` with the SAME doc shape ("`null` — the default — makes
     the plugin inert, which is what every host that is not a composer
     wants"); `punctuationLintPlugin(() => this.punctuationLint())` added
     LAST in `buildPlugins()`'s array (after the keymaps and
     `placeholderPlugin` — v4 mounts it last, `LexicalComposerWrapper.tsx:
     181-183`: "Read-only: paints warnings, never edits, so its order is
     immaterial"; in ProseMirror a decorations-only plugin is order-
     independent too — record); the refresh effect above; the plugin reads
     the input INSIDE the closure so a `null → value` flip needs no editor
     rebuild. `markdown-field.ts` / `document-pane.ts` pass nothing (inert).
   - **NEW `punctuation-lint-plugin.spec.ts`** — transcribe the 12 cases of
     `PunctuationLintPlugin.test.tsx` against a real `EditorView` in jsdom
     (the `smart-typography-plugin.spec.ts` / `rich-editor.spec.ts` rigs +
     `jsdom-range-shim.ts`): `paints a double quote opened inside open
     dialogue, and nothing else`; `never changes the text` (the doc's
     `textContent` and the serialized markdown unchanged after a paint);
     `clears the paint once the mistake is fixed`; `does not flag an opener
     the caret has just typed`; `maps offsets across text nodes and soft
     line breaks` (a `hard_break` + a marked run); `leaves code blocks
     alone` (a `code_block` and an inline `code` run); `paints nothing when
     the setting is off`; prose mode: `paints nothing of the kind outside
     prose mode`, `previews narration and asides, and flags a draft with no
     dialogue`, `drops the no-dialogue tint once a quote opens, keeping the
     preview`, `keeps the preview but drops the tint when warnings are off`,

## The differential requirement, restated for this lane

This lane owns NO cargo harness family and NO jest oracle; the sweep driver
(`harness/tools/recipe_sweep.py`) has nothing of yours to run and you run
it for nothing (one sweep at a time machine-wide — leave it to the lanes
that need it). Your equivalence tests are SPA vitest gates, three of them,
each driving v4's REAL code at the pin:

| unit | v4 code executed | recorder / recipe | v5 spec that replays it | tier |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| the punctuation engine + prose mode | `lib/punctuation-lint/engine.ts` + `prose-mode.ts` IMPORTED (through `git show 236c04e35:…` into a temp dir, Node 24 type stripping — no `node_modules`) | NEW `harness/oracle/cases/punctuation-lint-engine.mjs` → `apps/web/src/app/punctuation-lint/engine.v4.json` (the `concierge-chat-override.mjs` → `concierge-state.v4.json` recipe, P4.D229) | `punctuation-lint/engine.oracle.spec.ts` (every row, field by field; `_source.pin` + the row count pinned) | 1 (exact) |
| the two vector corpora | the fixtures ARE v4's contract ("quilltap-v5 copies this byte-for-byte") | `cp` from the pin; md5 in the Preamble | `engine.spec.ts` (35 cases) + `prose-mode.spec.ts` / the prose tables (10 + 8) — the `typography-vectors.json` family, P4.D74 unit 1 | 1 (exact) |
| the renderer (the Novel rows + the 67 existing) | `lib/services/markdown-renderer.service.ts` → `renderMarkdownToHtml` IMPORTED from the pin (needs the pin's `node_modules`) + `lib/chat/novel-template.ts`'s `NOVEL_TEMPLATE` | `apps/web/tooling/capture-markdown-fixtures.mts` run FROM the pin with `QT_V4_ROOT=$PIN` → `chat/render/__fixtures__/markdown-fixtures.json` (the P4.30 / P4.D74 recipe; `status-log.md:71145`) | `chat/render/markdown-renderer.spec.ts` (byte-for-byte per row) + `typography.spec.ts` | 1 (exact bytes) |

- **Silent-stale-pass discipline (ledger §5.2):** a corpus that was not
  regenerated proves nothing. Each of the three carries a freshness tell
  you assert: `engine.v4.json._source.pin === '236c04e35'` and its
  `engineMd5` equal to the Preamble's; the two vector files' md5 (a one-line
  `shasum`-style check in the lane record, not in the spec — the spec pins
  `cases.length`); `markdown-fixtures.json` containing the `template-novel-
  *` names AND the 67 pre-existing rows byte-stable (the diff you record).
  A `.v4.json` row count written as a literal into the spec is what stops a
  truncated corpus from passing vacuously (`concierge-state.oracle.spec.ts:
  52-56`'s shape).
- **Equivalent-mutant trap** (memory note `sweep-driver-recipe-header-
  rules`): Tier 1 item 2's deliberate one-rule flip is the proof the
  recorded corpus can FAIL; do the same once for the renderer (drop the
  `openTags` stack → the `-unmarked-narration-italic` row must go red) and
  record both counts.
- **IMPORT-DEAD re-aims this lane owns:** NONE. The survey's §5.1 census
  verified every oracle case in this area resolves at the pin; the
  `capture-markdown-fixtures.mts` import path (`lib/services/markdown-
  renderer.service.ts`) and the `novel-template.ts` import both exist at
  `236c04e35` (`git -C ~/source/quilltap-server ls-tree 236c04e35 -- <path>`
  — run it once and record).
- **What the unifier re-runs from ITS pin (§S.2):** both of your recorded
  corpora (`markdown-fixtures.json` "67 → ~90 rows, the existing 67 byte-
  stable"; `engine.v4.json` from "P4.D352's tsx leaf recorder" — your `.mjs`
  `git show` recorder IS that recorder; name it so in the lane record so
  the unifier finds the file) and byte-compares them with your committed
  files. A mismatch there is a drift finding, not yours to pre-empt.

## Rulings made at planning

Items the HUMAN must rule on are marked ⚖ and listed first; the rest are
the planner's calls (the human may overrule any before lane start — a STOP
item if they do).

- ⚖ **R-0 — the npm-cache gap (round-wide; vendor survey §F.4).** This lane
  needs the pin's root `node_modules` for EXACTLY one step — the renderer
  re-capture (Tier 1 item 7). `npm ci --offline` at the pin fails
  `ENOTCACHED` today. The planner's call is ONE online `npm ci` in a pin
  worktree before any lane starts; this order's reading: run the offline
  `npm ci` at lane start, REPORT on `ENOTCACHED`, continue the units that
  need no pin `node_modules`, and hold Tier 1's close on the capture. The
  alternative — capturing against the live checkout (`openai` 7.45.1, the
  v13 driver, a dirty tree) — is REFUSED by the round. The human may
  instead authorize this lane to run the online `npm ci` itself.
- ⚖ **R-4 — the `Draft formatting instructions` button stays a standing
  deferral** (`template-form-modal.ts:42-45`; round block R-Y: "P4.D352
  Tier 2 may lift it only if the human says so"). The planner's call:
  leave it (a TS twin of a function the Rust side ports and the SPA never
  calls — Tier 2 item 3 only on a yes). Alternative: lift it now while the
  template modal is open anyway — costs a second recorded corpus.
- ⚖ **R-7 — KaTeX at the pin is 0.19.1 at the ROOT; v5 pins 0.18.4.** The
  capture note (`capture-markdown-fixtures.mts:28-36`) says the math bytes
  come from `rehype-katex`'s own NESTED `katex` (0.16.47 in both trees at
  `48396682`), so the root bump should not move the `math-*` rows. If the
  re-capture moves ANY `math-*` row: STOP on those rows, report the diff,
  and do NOT bump v5's `katex` in this lane (`apps/web/package.json`'s deps
  are P4.D353's this round — a katex move would be a HANDOFF to it or the
  unifier's one-line bump + re-capture). Alternative: the human authorizes
  the bump in this lane (then record it in the lockfile-touching commit).
- **R-1 — the engine is a TS port checked tier-1 EXACT against v4's REAL
  engine** (the round's R-I / R-K): the byte-copied vectors are the floor;
  the recorded `engine.v4.json` from the IMPORTED pin engine is the proof;
  the port's source is a COPY of v4's file, so "port disagrees with v4"
  can only mean the copy was edited — fix the copy. Alternative (a hand
  transcription from the tests): refused — that is the pre-P4.D229 state
  the concierge twin left.
- **R-2 — the adapter is ProseMirror `Decoration.inline`, a RECORDED
  adapter divergence** (survey §8.2; the round block §R.7 names it): v4's
  CSS Custom Highlight API has no jsdom support, needs a document-global
  registry, and a decoration span gives the hover `title` for free; the
  four `::highlight(name)` rules become `.name` class rules with the SAME
  tokens, declared in ascending v4 priority so the cascade stands in for
  `Highlight.priority`. The record names what is NOT ported (Tier 3 items
  1–4). Alternative (keep `CSS.highlights` — available in the Tauri WebKit
  webview and Chromium 105+): a lane may choose it for fidelity, but then
  the component spec cannot run in jsdom and the `unavailable` debug line
  and the registry must be ported too — refused for this round.
- **R-3 — `narrowRenderingPatterns`'s coercion and `typography.ts:126`'s
  missing `mentionsGlyph` exemption are v5 DEFECTS fixed FIRST, red-first,
  and recorded as such — not drift** (round block R-Y; survey §0 items 2–3).
  The lane record names them under "v5 gaps retired".
- **R-5 — the gated beats ride the three EXISTING shared-server specs
  behind `P4D341_LANDED` / `P4D351_SERVER_LANDED` / `P4D356_SERVER_LANDED`
  (ACTIVATE-AT-UNIFY, §S.9)**, the Novel template CREATED through the UI

  - (h) **The keystone-memory facts the row overstated:** `keystone` is a
    FLAG, not a tier (R-E) — `bucketCandidates` skips `memory.keystone`
    rows AFTER `rowsLoaded++`, BEFORE the `CONSOLIDATED` branch; `isDemotable
    = hot && AUTO && !keystone`; `policyProtectionOf` answers `'keystone'`
    FIRST and `sparedBy`'s key order becomes `{keystone, manual, digest,
    cold}`; a keystone fold survivor takes NO footnotes and an EMPTY patch
    but keeps links + the reinforcement delta; `cascadeEmptiedDigests` keeps
    a keystone digest whose last member retires (DEBUG `Keystone digest kept
    though its last member retires`) and its `added` now counts only rows
    actually doomed (`if (!grew) break`) — port as shipped; `protection()`
    answers `{score: 1, protected: true, protectedBySource: true}`. In
    recall, "every digest is cut before any keystone" holds only INSIDE the
    inner circle (`innerCircleTrimOrder` sorts `Number(b.keystone) −
    Number(a.keystone)` first): present-character keystones are LIFTED into
    the circle at `tier −1` ahead of that character's digests, self
    keystones join the anchors, and `renderArchive` / `renderHead` render a
    keystone's `content` first (`summary` first otherwise). `KEYSTONES_PER_
    SUBJECT_MAX = 5`; the v4 quirk — `POST /memories` with `keystone: true`
    answers 400 `The memory was saved, but not as a keystone: <msg>` AFTER
    writing the row — is ported as the bytes (Q3). `keystone` appears in
    EVERY parsed Memory (between `consolidatedAt` and `createdAt`), so every
    byte pin of a memory row moves; `hydrateRawRows` coerces number / bigint
    / null to a boolean and DROPS a string cell with the WARN.

    own commit; the unifier checks the handoff list. Ties go to the feature
    lane (the a11y edits are mechanical)." Every sibling SPA lane applies
    rules **R1–R12** (the binding text is Shared contract C9) to the files it
    owns and records each as a `HANDOFF:` for P4.D358's census; P4.D358's
    static census script takes an allowlist for sibling-owned files, which
    the unifier empties. Already CONVERGED (not reds): the sidebar switches,
    `Allow Any Character`, the edit box, the composer `Message`, the
    slide-over's focus. The real gap: of 34 overlay components, 13 have no
    `role="dialog"`; `qt-modal` (77 hosts) carries `role` + `aria-modal` +
    the close label but NO `aria-labelledby` / `tabindex` / focus / Escape.

- **§R.5 — Log lines.** Every line this round ports or moves lands with a
  capture pin (the thread-scoped rig) AND its silence leg.
  - A line emitted INSIDE a `Db::write` closure runs on the writer thread and
    is INVISIBLE to the rig: run the home on the caller thread (memory note
    `capture-rig-writer-thread`) or use `global_capture`.
  - A `db::fallback` line renders the BARE SQLite / Zod message
    (`error_text`), never `sqlite error: …`.
  - A structured v4 field that is an array or object renders through the
    `…Json` file-layer convention; v4's camelCase keys are the keys; an
    `undefined` v4 field is OMITTED; child-logger bindings are fields on the
    line; `durationMs` and other wall-clock fields are MASKED.
  - Open before `captured_with`; a v4 catch UNREACHABLE through v4's real
    code is pinned NEGATIVE with a v5 fallback home.
  - **Migration lines follow the standing precedent** (P4.D249 / P4.D304 /
    P4.D315 / P4.D324, `host.rs`): v4's migration INFO / ERROR texts are
    NO-PORT and each boot ensure logs ONE v5-worded INFO per added column /
    table / index.

- **§R.7 — Red-first.**
  - **Measure red before touching source** — on unported `main` for the
    wave-0 lanes, on the `KEYSTONE` tip for its dependents (a contract
    refusal arm answering `not_available` IS the red). The lane record
    carries the red count per family. A test that passes before the fix is
    a test of nothing.
  - **Ruled divergences** are NAMED both-ways tables in the idiom of
    `FRESH_STORE_RESIDUAL` / `BOOT_CREATED_HISTORY_TABLE`, so a v4
    convergence trips them. This round's planned names: **P4.D345**
    `HOLD_KEEP_ALIVE_NO_STREAM` (v4's 15 s keep-alive ping — no v5
    counterpart by architecture), `ROUND_DROPS_MIN_EXCLUSIVE` (only if the
    corpus shows it); **P4.D354** `REFUSING_SEAM_LEAVES_PDF_UNEXTRACTED`
    (the backfill and the `_indexed` bridges leave a PDF / DOCX row at
    `'none'` while `DocumentTextExtractor::is_available()` is `false`; the
    REST path keeps v4's `failed` bytes); **P4.D347** `ECHO_SLICE_SURROGATE`
    + `SENTENCE_SLICE_SURROGATE` (a JS `slice` that cuts a surrogate pair —
    v5 rounds to the code-point boundary); **P4.D343** the dry scope's
    table-delta proof (whatever the `DryBuildContextSeams` wrapper does not
    cover is a recorded divergence, never a silent write) and `plugin_tool_
    effects` as NO-COUNTERPART; **P4.D350** `PUBLISH_AFTER_COMMIT` only if
    the human overrules its R-1 (v4 publishes at the write; v5 after the
    commit); **P4.D355** the explicit `terminal_sessions` DELETE on chat
    delete (v5's cascade stand-in on a generateDDL table with no FK — named
    in its record; R-R); **P4.D341** none owed (Q3's post-write 400 is
    ported as bytes); **P4.D352** an ADAPTER divergence recorded in the SPA
    (ProseMirror `Decoration.inline` for v4's CSS Custom Highlight API; the
    four `::highlight(name)` rules become class rules with the same tokens);
    **P4.D353** a Tier-3 code-block note only if R-B is overruled to (C).

    (**P4.D360**). ONE convergence row in the span: `7789ebed6` (#120 — this
    port's F-1 filing); bugs 258–269 are v4-originated.
- **§R.8 — The unifier's accumulation.** Each lane's record states its
  `Cargo.toml` versions at close; the unifier takes the HIGHEST and bumps +1
  for the union commit; `Cargo.lock` follows. The SPA: the highest of the
  SPA lanes, +1. CHANGELOG entries and `status-log.md` lane records
  union-merge (append-only).
- **§R.9 — One machine.**
  - **Workspace gates:** at most TWO Rust workspace gates at once. Count them
    with ONE `ps -ax -o pid,etime,command | grep "cargo test --workspace" |
    grep -v grep`; if two run, queue yours in ONE background command waiting
    on the siblings' PIDs (`while kill -0 <pidA> && kill -0 <pidB>; do sleep
    20; done; sleep $((RANDOM % 20)); <re-count once; if still two, wait on
    those PIDs>; cargo test …`), never on a pattern (memory notes
    `no-pattern-kill-on-shared-machine`, `parallel-lane-shared-resources`
    §2 — co-waiters all fire on one exit; re-count after the wait).
  - **Sweeps and Playwright:** one sweep at a time (announced); one
    Playwright run at a time (lanes by file only; the full suite is the
    unifier's). New own-instance e2e files' server ports are unique per
    lane (4319–4340, 4343–4350, 4352 and mocks 45301–45304, 45340–45348 are
    taken): **P4.D344 4353 / mock 45351**, **P4.D346 4354 / mock 45352**,
    **P4.D352 4355 / mock 45353**, **P4.D353 4356 / mock 45354**, **P4.D358
    4357 / mock 45355**, **P4.D359 4358 / mock 45356**, **P4.D355 4359 /
    mock 45357** (only if a terminal e2e is added). A lane that only edits
    an EXISTING spec takes no port.
  - **Long jobs:** `run_in_background: true`, full log in a lane-private
    file, awaited by the completion notification — NEVER a `pgrep`/`grep`
    poll loop (CLAUDE.md), never `tail -N` on a gate log. A gate reads its
    oracles and source at RUN time: no regen and no source edit while one
    runs (memory note `lane-gate-hygiene`).
  - **Targets:** clone main's `target/` before the first build (`cp -cR`;
    `/carryout` rule 8 — same volume only, memory note
    `apfs-clone-same-volume-only`); main's `target/` was warmed by the
    `/setupphase` chain on 2026-10-29 at the orders' commit (the test /
    clippy set the lanes clone; the release bins were swept — an
    own-instance e2e that prefers `target/release/*` must delete the stale
    release bins from its clone or build release, memory note
    `lane-spa-env-node-modules-and-release-bins`); a stacked lane may clone
    its parent lane's worktree target instead, when that lane is not
    mid-build.
  - **`apps/web/dist/`:** a fresh worktree has none — build it before any
    workspace clippy / test. **`apps/web/node_modules`:** `npm ci` in the
    lane (a cloned copy fails every vitest spec with NG0203).
  - **Concurrency:** the cap is the two-gate rule, not the lane count;
    twenty lanes do NOT all run at once (§S.0 waves). Disk at planning:
    144 GiB free on the repo volume with `target/` at 347 GB BEFORE the
    warm sweep — the human sizes the waves from the post-sweep numbers in
    the hand-off report.

  start (an overruled ruling is a STOP item for that lane). The round-level
  ones to read FIRST — **APPROVED AS PLANNED by the human at the hand-off
  (2026-10-29): the npm-cache step (ONE online `npm ci` in a pin worktree
  before lane start), the pin `236c04e35`, R-B option A (the four diagram
  deps), the three SPA convergences (R-N), the `@axe-core/playwright` dev

- **§R.14 — Stacking rules.** Each named stacking commit — `KEYSTONE`
  (P4.D341), `CONTEXT` (P4.D347), `SPINE` (P4.D348), `CATALOG` (P4.D350's
  FIRST commit), `FILES` (P4.D354) — is ONE commit whose subject starts with
  its name; the owner records the sha in its status header ON ITS LANE
  BRANCH the moment it lands. A dependent lane — P4.D347 / P4.D342 / P4.D351
  / P4.D356 from `KEYSTONE`; P4.D348 from `CONTEXT`; P4.D343 / P4.D345 from
  `SPINE` (which is on `CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`); P4.D349 from `CATALOG`;
  P4.D355 from `FILES` — finds the sha per `/carryout` rule 2 (`git log --all
  --format='%h %s' | grep -E '^[0-9a-f]+ <NAME>:'`, confirmed against the
  parent's lane-branch copy of the order) and branches from it — never from
  `main`, never a guessed sha; if the parent has not recorded it, STOP and
  report. A stacked lane may PRE-START from `main` on work that calls none
  of its parent's contract names — P4.D342's prompt bytes (the four
  extraction ladders + the consolidation tail) + the inner-circle resolver
  (`services/inner_circle.rs`, the tuning knobs); P4.D343's `tools/effects.rs`
  + `pascal/tool_effect.rs` + `StreamParams.tool_choice` + the `tool-build`
  re-aim (its unit E) + the pure `storyteller/*` modules; P4.D345's hold
  registry + eligibility / overrides / recompute / spans + the engine's
  `ReplayInputs` + the ONE corpus regen + the schema re-vendor; P4.D351's
  `services/annotations.rs` + `template_prompt_hint.rs` ports; P4.D356's
  NanoGPT half + the Tier R verify; P4.D349's `pronoun-presets` /
  `pronoun-guidance` ports + the image-prompt `humanoid` threading; P4.D355's
  `db/log_entries.rs` + the terminal host; P4.D348's `operator_post.rs` /
  `whisper_send.rs` / `turn_control.rs` additions and the funnel recorder;
  P4.D347 has no pre-start (its first unit widens `RawMessage`, which rides
  the keystone's row shapes) — rebasing onto the parent's commit before the
  first commit that does call a contract name; that rebase is the one
  sanctioned exception to "no rebase mid-lane". Two sibling-stacked parts
  (not lane-named commits): P4.D346's grammar-twin part (`pascal/custom-tool-
  types.ts` + the corpus copy + the spec header) stacks on P4.D345's Tier-1
  commit, or authors blind and lets the unifier run `custom-tool-types.
  corpus.spec.ts` — the order states which; P4.D359's chips stack on
  P4.D353's Tier-1 commit (the `message-content.ts` hook). Every other lane
  cuts from `main` at the orders' commit.

## §S — Unifier wires (what no single lane can do)

- **§S.0 — Waves (the recommended execution; the two-gate cap rules the
  machine, not the lane count).**
  - **Wave 0, from the orders' commit, at once:** P4.D341 (→ `KEYSTONE`,
    the Opus / Fable tier), P4.D350 (→ `CATALOG` as its FIRST commit, then
    its own units), P4.D354 (→ `FILES`), and in parallel P4.D360, P4.D357,
    P4.D358. That is six worktrees.
  - **Wave 1, as slots free (still from the orders' commit):** the five SPA
    feature lanes P4.D344, P4.D346, P4.D352, P4.D353, P4.D359 (each authors
    against the contracts with gated beats; P4.D346's grammar-twin part and
    P4.D359's chips wait for their sibling's Tier-1 commit).
  - **Wave A, from `KEYSTONE`:** P4.D347 (→ `CONTEXT`) FIRST, then P4.D342,
    P4.D351, P4.D356 (its NanoGPT + Tier R half may have PRE-STARTED from
    `main`). **From `CATALOG`:** P4.D349 — FIRST among the independents
    (R-H). **From `FILES`:** P4.D355.
  - **Wave B, from `CONTEXT`:** P4.D348 (→ `SPINE`).
  - **Wave C, from `SPINE`:** P4.D343 and P4.D345 (the two turn-side
    consumers every later union depends on).

  Within a wave every lane compiles against the C sections below it alone.
- **§S.1 — The pick order.** P4.D341 → P4.D350 → P4.D349 → P4.D347 →
  P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 → P4.D342 → P4.D351 → P4.D356 → P4.D354 →
  P4.D355 → P4.D357 → P4.D344 → P4.D346 → P4.D352 → P4.D353 → P4.D359 →
  P4.D360 → P4.D358. The reasons per adjacency: `KEYSTONE` first (every
  server lane's row shapes, the dumps, the ensures); P4.D350 next because
  `CATALOG` is the base P4.D349 branches from, and P4.D349 right after it
  (R-H — the three LIVE-DATA erasure writers land before anything else
  touches a vault); P4.D347 → P4.D348 → P4.D343 → P4.D345 in their stacking
  order (`CONTEXT` on `KEYSTONE`, `SPINE` on `CONTEXT`, the two consumers of
  S1 / S2 / C1 / `TurnStopReach` on `SPINE`; P4.D343 before P4.D345 so
  `trigger_runner.rs`'s pre-run ceiling block precedes the post-run `Vetoed`
  arm and `realtime/types.rs` lands `storytellerRounds` NINTH before
  `customToolInterventions` TENTH); P4.D342 and P4.D351 (keystone-only
  consumers, no spine seam) after the spine chain; P4.D356 after P4.D345 so
  the `customToolIntervention` arm it writes for P4.D345 and the
  `punctuationWarnings` key it writes for P4.D352 are in the tree when their
  families re-run, and after P4.D351 so `settings-routes` is regenerated
  ONCE at the end of the server picks; P4.D354 → P4.D355 (`FILES` then the
  two bridge sites switched); P4.D357 before every SPA lane (the CSS lines
  the fenced appends sit beside); P4.D344 before P4.D346 (`_index.css`
  imports in v4's order; `RealtimeTopicAppend`'s SPA half NINTH then TENTH;
  `LibraryEntryKeys`' SPA badges in v4's order); P4.D352 before P4.D353
  (`renderMarkdownToHtml` is P4.D352's; P4.D353 post-passes its HTML);
  P4.D353 before P4.D359 (P4.D359's chips stack on P4.D353's `message-
  content.ts` hook; its `_variables.css` append sits after P4.D353's);
  P4.D360 LATE (its help / docs guards judge a union carrying every server
  feature; its schema-guard CHECK sees the final constants; the P4.D339
  precedent: fourteenth) — nineteenth here, before P4.D358; P4.D358 LAST
  (its census allowlist for sibling-owned HANDOFF files is emptied against
  the finished union; its toast-selector first commit is cherry-picked
  EARLIER by any sibling that edits one of the 20 spec files).

  §1 is restated at the close probe: the regen rule (PIN REQUIRED at the
  new baseline — v4 HEAD is already past it); the schema state (moves
  17–21 LANDED in ONE re-dump; `migration_indexes.json` +3); the LIVE-DATA
  block (vendor survey §I, sharpened by the keystone survey — **a v5 at
  `236c04e35` reads and writes:** `memories.keystone` (the 17th move, the
  migration's `INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0` + the partial index `idx_memories_
  keystone` as ONE boot ensure), `chats.turnMode` / `storytellerInviteFilter`,
  `chat_messages.storytellerBid`, the NEW table `storyteller_rounds` (with
  its FK + both indexes as ensures), `llm_logs.storytellerRoundId` (the
  FIRST llm-logs column ensure v5 has needed — before the structural
  verify), `chat_settings.customToolIntervention` (default ON), `roleplay_
  templates.unmarkedStyle` / `markdownAsterisks` — and runs `778b97b2b`'s
  every-boot seed that re-encodes the built-in templates' `narrationDelimiters`
  as JSON and inserts the Novel template with it `null`. **The first v5
  boot of a pre-`8cb584eff` Friday copy ADDS the eight columns + one table
  (column-gated, v4's shapes, every ensure UNSTAMPED) and REWRITES the
  built-in templates' delimiter cells `*` → `"*"`; a copy v4 migrated sees
  NO-OP ensures and a no-op seed (measure: the seed must be idempotent
  against v4's own output).** **The THREE vault-key ERASURE hazards RETIRE**
  once P4.D349 lands: `render_physical_prompts_json` carries `humanoid` (a
  v4 `"humanoid": false` survives a v5 physical write and the P4.82
  backfill respects it), `PropertiesJson` carries `canDrawDiagrams` /
  `diagramStyle`, `build_wardrobe_item_file` carries `pronounGuidance`;
  until that lane is on the union build, **no v5 write to a character v4
  edited past `6588972d5` / `b27e5089a` / `eb90d9ed8`** (reads stay safe).
  **The Log is carried** (`cc5f2347c`, P4.D355): a v5 backup writes
  `data/log-entries.json`, a `replace` restore / delete-all clears
  `log_entries`, a `new-account` restore remaps `id` / `chatId` /
  `characterId`; `.qtap` still omits it; a pre-round v5 backup has no Log
  file — restoring it on the union leaves the Log empty (expected).
  **Keystones protected:** maintenance, demotion, consolidation, fold and
  chat-wipe skip `keystone = 1` rows; recall lifts them into the circle; a
  v5 backup / `.qtap` carries the column; a pre-round v5 build must NOT
  run maintenance on a copy v4 keystoned. **Terminals:** kill sends SIGHUP
  then SIGKILL after 3 s; a deleted chat terminates its shells (and v5
  deletes its `terminal_sessions` rows explicitly); the daily sweep unlinks
  orphaned transcripts older than 1 h (`85c002822`, P4.D355). **Settings:**
  the chat-settings PUT refuses unknown keys with a 400 (`3b8b6f42b`) — a
  v5 SPA caller that echoed a GET row back now fails (P4.D356's census).
  **Files:** ingest fills `doc_mount_blobs.extractedText*` for native-text
  uploads and a boot backfill fills old rows (`40fab1c54`, P4.D354 — a DATA
  WRITE on first boot of any copy with uploads: measure the row count; PDF
  / DOCX rows stay `'none'` under the refusing seam). **The general rule
  BITES: a v5 build from BEFORE this round must not open an instance this
  round's ensures have altered — the owed dogfood pass runs with the UNION
  build.** **NOT this round (`8832cfd20`, `bf7a457f6`):** v4 HEAD links
  SQLite3MC 2.4.0 / SQLite 3.53.4; v5 stays 2.3.5 / 3.53.2 — the first v5
  open of a post-`8832cfd20` Friday copy is the measurement; the iCloud
  placeholder scan divergence stands as recorded. **Standing:** no v5 build
  against live Friday; dogfood a COPY; do not round-trip v4-written
  characters through v5 writes while comparing with v4); the dependency
  facts (`openai` 7.45.0, `@openrouter/sdk` 1.5.9, `pdfjs-dist` 6.4.299,
  `pdf-parse` removed, `@quilltap/plugin-types` 2.9.0; the four client deps
  `mermaid` 12.2.0 / `@hpcc-js/wasm-graphviz` 1.30.0 / `svg2roughjs` 3.2.3 /
  `dompurify` 3.4.17 if R-B lands them; `@axe-core/playwright` if R-N does);
  `help/` 137; `docs/v4` 518.

- **§S.9 — The gated beats flip LIVE.** P4.D344 / P4.D346 / P4.D352 /

  (`const P4D343_SERVER_LANDED = false` etc.), with throwaway stand-ins

  before it is accepted as the recorded timing class.

## Shared contracts (binding; name-level — the long forms are the surveys' §7 and, where an order is finished, that order's contract section)

- **C2 — the Storyteller wire W1 (P4.D343 builds, P4.D344 consumes;
  `crates/quilltap-core/src/api/types.rs` + `apps/web/src/app/core/core-
  contract.ts`):** `Request::ChatUpdate { …, turn_mode: Option<Option<Value>>
  }` (`double_option`, serde key `turnMode`, a sibling of `chat`; absent →
  untouched; `"rotation"` / `"storyteller"` → `set_turn_mode` (+ `abandon_
  storyteller_round(chat_id, "abandoned")` on leaving); `null` or anything
  else → `BadRequest "Validation error"`, nothing written) ↔ `ChatUpdate
  Request.turnMode?: 'rotation' | 'storyteller'`; the bag key `chat.
  storytellerInviteFilter: boolean` (non-boolean → the whole PUT refused).
  `Request::ChatStorytellerRound { chat_id }` → `Response::ChatStoryteller
  Round(Value)` = `{ "round": StorytellerRound | null }`. `Request::Chat
  StorytellerRun { chat_id, round_id: String, participant_ids: Vec<String>,
  order: Option<String>, keep_order: Option<bool> }` (keys `roundId`,
  `participantIds`, `order` default `"sequential"`, `keepOrder`; a non-enum
  `order` → `BadRequest "Invalid request"`) → an EMPTY reply (`Response::Ok`
  / `Null` — the web edge answers the SSE stream; `{ ended }` would be extra
  information v4 never answers); refusals `Conflict` 409 `That round is no
  longer open`, `NotFound` 404 `That seat is not on this round` / `Chat not
  found`, `BadRequest` 400 `Choose at least one bid, or let the round lie` /
  `Only a seat that bid to speak can be chosen` / `A Destroy bid must be
  chosen on its own`. `Request::ChatStorytellerAsk { chat_id, round_id,
  participant_id }` → `Response::ChatStorytellerAsk(Value)` = `{ "bid":
  StorytellerBid }` (the ANSWERED card). `Request::ChatStorytellerLie {
  chat_id, round_id }` → `{ "closed": true }`. Frames on `EventPayload::Chat
  (ChatEvent)`: `{"storytellerRound": StorytellerRound}`, `{"storytellerBid":
  {"roundId", "bid": StorytellerBid}}`, `{"roundOpen": true, "roundId"}`,
  `chainComplete` with `reason: "round_open"` (the literal; no enum change);
  a concurrent turn's frames carry `"participantId"` (when the frame lacked
  one) and `"concurrent": true` as the LAST two keys (`ChatEvent::Tagged`
  flattening inner then the two keys). The chat GET wire: `turnMode:
  'rotation' | 'storyteller'` (never null on the wire), `storytellerInvite
  Filter: boolean`, both after `isPaused`. Realtime topic `"storytellerRounds"`
  scoped by chat id → `chatKeys.storytellerRound(chatId) = ['chat', chatId,
  'storytellerRound']`. `LlmLogDto.storytellerRoundId?: string | null`; log
  type `"STORYTELLER_BID"`. `CustomToolLibraryEntry.effect: 'read' | 'write'`
  after `intervention` (T2). TS types `StorytellerBid` (key order
  `participantId, characterId, characterName, status, statedTier?, tier?,
  tierRaised, tools, plan?, reason?, talkativeness, silent, askedAt?,
  answeredAt?`), `StorytellerRound` (`id, chatId, triggerMessageId, status,
  bids, selection, closedReason, createdAt, updatedAt, closedAt`),
  `StorytellerSelection { participantIds, order: Order | null, chosenAt,
  names }`, `StorytellerBidRecord { roundId, tier, plan, toolsNamed }`,
  `StorytellerTier` (`simple | lookup | act | destroy`), `StorytellerOrder`
  (`sequential | simultaneous`), `TIER_LABELS` — field for field, optional /
  nullable preserved. P4.D344 codes to this with `it.skipIf(SERVER_LANDED)`
  gates.

    and back to the opener on close, NO Tab trap, close button `aria-label=
    "Close"`, Escape inside closes it (topmost: `stopPropagation`), Escape
    elsewhere closes it only while `!document.querySelector('[aria-modal=
    "true"]')`, both skipping `defaultPrevented`.
  - **R3 Icon-only buttons** carry v4's `aria-label` byte: `Close`, `Cancel`,
    `Dismiss message`, `Close gallery`, `` Remove alias ${alias} ``, `Up one
    folder`, `Toggle model list` (+ `aria-expanded`).
  - **R4 Visible label ↔ control:** `<label for="<id>">` + `id="<id>"` on the
    control; never a bare `<span>` caption beside a control.
  - **R5 Heading-captioned control:** `aria-labelledby="<headingId>"` with
    `id` on the heading (ProfilesTab / ImageGenerationCard / ModelBehaviorCard
    / FieldSelectionStep / SuggestionCard / ContextCompression switch).
  - **R6 Placeholder-only / caption-less control:** `aria-label` with v4's
    string (never invent a string; where v4 has none, do not add one).
  - **R7 Switch:** `role="switch"` + `aria-checked` + a name (`aria-label` or
    `aria-labelledby`); the Prospero one is `type="button"` too.
  - **R8 Clickable non-button:** `role="button" tabindex="0" (keydown)=
    "activateOnKey($event)"` (P4.D358's `ui/activate-on-key.ts`) with
    `aria-expanded` (FileTable) / `aria-pressed` (gallery tile, quick-hide
    tags) where v4 sets them. Prefer a real `<button>` where the layout
    allows (v4 kept spans only inside `<tr>` / heading contexts and under
    the uppercase-button themes).
  - **R9 Live region for notices:** the toast container `role="status"
    aria-live="polite" data-toast-container`; an error toast `role="alert"`
    (P4.D358 only).
  - **R10 Colour-only state gets words:** `<span class="sr-only">Unmoderated
    chat.</span>`; ` (overheard)` appended to the whisper label.
  - **R11 Async result announced:** `<span class="sr-only" role="status"
    aria-live="polite">…</span>` (DocumentPane: `editingIndicator ??
    (saveLabel === 'Saved' ? 'Saved' : '')`).
  - **R12 Pointer targets ≥ 24 px** — CSS, P4.D357 (`.qt-tab-close`);
    templates: `min-w-6 min-h-6` on the inline copy-id button (P4.D358).
